2026-11-04T15:04:16.6557660Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Submit round 41: pinned B/C and the MegaMoE scale-path upper-bound probe

Run normal PP4 serving with dense first-K, QK-norm fixed attention and the
one-pass div448 kernels all pinned, so no small-kernel adoption gate runs.
The freed window before Prefill startup measures, on all twelve Prefill
GPUs, production MegaMoE minus a diagnostic variant that skips the
split-loop rescale. The median delta per layer is an upper bound on the
whole scale path. Below 50 microseconds per layer closes the mainloop
redesign direction; at or above 50 keeps it open only conditionally.

The build is required because the DeepGEMM host extension gains the
separate diagnostic entry; the serving entry is hardwired to the
production template. The probe never writes serving settings.
